           format!(\"{}KB\", *size / 1024)\n368\t        } else {\n369\t            format!(\"{}B\", *size)\n370\t        };\n371\t        eprintln!(\"  {}: {}\", size_label, stats.format());\n372\t    }\n373\t}\n374\t\n375\t/// Measures and reports median latency for a 100KB trace.\n376\t///\n377\t/// This function performs multiple iterations and returns the sorted\n378\t/// latency measurements. Used by both the criterion benchmark and the\n379\t/// assertion-style test.\n380\t#[allow(dead_code)]\n381\tfn measure_median_latency_100kb() -> (Vec<u128>, u128) {\n382\t    let sanitizer = Sanitizer::new(&[]).expect(\"failed to build sanitizer\");\n383\t    let content = generate_trace_content(SIZE_100KB);\n384\t    let sample_count = std::env::var(\"SANITIZER_BENCH_SAMPLE_COUNT\")\n385\t        .ok()\n386\t        .and_then(|s| s.parse().ok())\n387\t        .unwrap_or(ASSERTION_SAMPLE_COUNT);\n388\t\n389\t    let mut latencies = Vec::with_capacity(sample_count);\n390\t\n391\t    for _ in 0..sample_count {\n392\t        let start = std::time::Instant::now();\n393\t        let _ = sanitizer.sanitize(&content);\n394\t        let elapsed_ms = start.elapsed().as_millis();\n395\t        latencies.push(elapsed_ms);\n396\t    }\n397\t\n398\t    latencies.sort();\n399\t    let median = latencies[sample_count / 2];\n400\t\n401\t    (latencies, median)\n402\t}\n403\t\n404\t/// Assertion-style test that fails if median latency exceeds threshold.\n405\t///\n406\t/// This test runs independently of criterion (which is expensive) and\n407\t/// can be run in CI to enforce the performance requirement.\n408\t///\n409\t/// To run:\n410\t/// ```bash\n411\t/// cargo test --bench sanitize -- --nocapture assertion_test\n412\t/// ```\n413\t#[allow(dead_code)]\n414\tfn assertion_test() {\n415\t    let (latencies, median) = measure_median_latency_100kb();\n416\t    let threshold = latency_threshold_ms();\n417\t\n418\t    // .ok()\n        .and_then(|s| s.parse().ok())\n        .unwrap_or(ASSERTION_SAMPLE_COUNT);\n\n    let mut latencies = Vec::with_capacity(sample_count);\n\n    for _ in 0..sample_count {\n        let data.get(\"template_version\").and_then(|v| v.as_str());\n195\t\n196\t                if let (Some(bead_id), Some(name), Some(version)) =\n197\t                    (bead_id, template_name, (name.to_string(), version.to_string()));\n214\t                    self.pending_dispatch_start\n215\t                        .insert(bead_id.to_string(), (name.to_string(), version.to_string()));\n216\t                }\n217\t            }\n218\t\n219\t            Some(\"agent.completed\") => {\n220\t                let bead_id = data.get(\"bead_id\").and_then(|v| }\n230\t                    }\n231\t                }\n232\t            }\n233\t\n234\t            Some(\"outcome.classified\") => {\n235\t                let bead_id = self.stats\n261\t            .iter()\n262\t            .map(|(template_name, by_version)| {\n263\t                let comparison = VariantComparison {\n264\t                    template_name: events have been observed for that template.\n275\t    pub fn comparison_for(&self, template_name: &str) -> Option<VariantComparison> {\n276\t        self.stats\n277\t            .get(template_name)\n278\t            .map(|by_version| VariantComparison {\n279\t                template_name: template_name.to_string(),\n280\t                min_dispatches: self.min_dispatches,\n281\t                variants: by_version.clone(),\n282\t            })\n283\t    }\n284\t}\n285\t\n286\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n287\t// Percentile helpers\n288\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n289\t\n290\t/// Calculate the 95th percentile from a slice of values.\n291\t///\n292\t/// The 95th percentile (p95) is the value below which 95% of observations\n293\t/// fall. It is commonly used to understand the \"tail\" of a distribution\n294\t/// — for example, in latency metrics, p95 tells you that 95% of requests\n295\t/// completed within this time, while the slowest 5% took longer.\n296\t///\n297\t/// # Edge Cases\n298\t///\n299\t/// This function handles all edge cases gracefully:\n300\t///\n301\t/// - **Empty slice**: Returns `0` (no data available)\n302\t/// - **Single element**: Returns that element (the only value available)\n303\t/// - **Two elements**: Uses linear interpolation to estimate p95 between the two values\n304\t/// - **Small samples (2-3 elements)**: Linear interpolation provides a reasonable estimate\n305\t///\n306\t/// All edge cases return sensible results without panicking.\n307\t///\n308\t/// # Algorithm\n309\t///\n310\t/// This function uses **linear interpolation**, which is the same method\n311\t/// used by Criterion.rs and is more accurate than the nearest-rank method:\n312\t///\n313\t/// 1. If the slice is empty, return 0 (no data case)\n314\t/// 2. If the slice has one element, return that element\n315\t/// 3. Sort the values in ascending order\n316\t/// 4. Calculate the rank: `rank = 0.95 * (n - 1)` where `n` is the number of elements\n317\t/// 5. Split the rank into integer and fractional parts\n318\t/// 6. Return the linear interpolation: `floor_value + (ceiling_value - floor_value) * fraction`\n319\t/// 7. Round to the nearest integer for the final result\n320\t///\n321\t/// This method was chosen because:\n322\t/// - **Accurate**: Uses linear interpolation like Criterion.rs for smooth percentile estimates\n323\t/// - **Standard**: Matches the behavior of common benchmarking libraries\n324\t/// - **Well-documented**: The algorithm is described in statistical literature\n325\t/// - **Deterministic**: Always produces the same result for the same input\n326\t/// - **Handles all sample sizes**: Works correctly from 0 to very large datasets\n327\t///\n328\t/// Note: This uses linear interpolation, not nearest-rank. For example, with 10 elements\n329\t/// `[10, 20, ..., 100]`, the 95th percentile is approximately `95.5` (rounded to `96`),\n330\t/// not the maximum value `100`.\n331\t///\n332\t/// # Examples\n333\t///\n334\t/// ## Basic usage with sorted data\n335\t///\n336\t/// ```\n337\t/// use needle::stats::calculate_p95;\n338\t///\n339\t/// let latencies = vec![10u128, 20, 30, 40, 50, 60, 70, 80, 90, 100];\n340\t/// let p95 = calculate_p95(&latencies);\n341\t/// // rank = 0.95 * 9 = 8.55, floor_index = 8, fraction = 0.55\n342\t/// // floor_value = 90, ceiling_value = 100\n343\t/// // interpolated = 90 + 10 * 0.55 = 95.5 → 96\n344\t/// assert_eq!(p95, 96);\n345\t/// ```\n346\t///\n347\t/// ## Works with unsorted input (sorts internally)\n348\t///\n349\t/// ```\n350\t/// use needle::stats::calculate_p95;\n351\t///\n352\t/// let unsorted = vec![100u128, 10, 50, 30, 70, 40, 60, 20, 80, 90];\n353\t/// let p95 = calculate_p95(&unsorted);\n354\t/// assert_eq!(p95, 96); // Function sorts internally → same result\n355\t/// ```\n356\t///\n357\t/// ## Larger dataset\n358\t///\n359\t/// ```\n360\t/// use needle::stats::calculate_p95;\n361\t///\n362\t/// // 20 elements: rank = 0.95 * 19 = 18.05, floor_index = 18, fraction = 0.05\n363\t/// let data = vec![10u128, 20, 30, 40, 50, 60, 70, 80, 90, 100,\n364\t///                  110, 120, 130, 140, 150, 160, 170, 180, 190, 200];\n365\t/// let p95 = calculate_p95(&data);\n366\t/// // floor_value = 190, ceiling_value = 200\n367\t/// // interpolated = 190 + 10 * 0.05 = 190.5 → 191\n368\t/// assert_eq!(p95, 191);\n369\t/// ```\n370\t///\n371\t/// ## Single element (degenerate case)\n372\t///\n373\t/// ```\n374\t/// use needle::stats::calculate_p95;\n375\t///\n376\t/// let single = vec![42u128];\n377\t/// let p95 = calculate_p95(&single);\n378\t/// assert_eq!(p95, 42); // Only value available\n379\t/// ```\n380\t///\n381\t/// ## Empty input\n382\t///\n383\t/// ```\n384\t/// use needle::stats::calculate_p95;\n385\t///\n386\t/// let empty: Vec<u128> = vec![];\n387\t/// let p95 = calculate_p95(&empty);\n388\t/// assert_eq!(p95, 0); // No data → returns 0\n389\t/// ```\n390\t///\n391\t/// ## Two elements (small sample)\n392\t///\n393\t/// ```\n394\t/// use needle::stats::calculate_p95;\n395\t///\n396\t/// let two = vec![10u128, 20];\n397\t/// let p95 = calculate_p95(&two);\n398\t/// // rank = 0.95 * 1 = 0.95, floor_index = 0, fraction = 0.95\n399\t/// // floor_value = 10, ceiling_value = 20\n400\t/// // interpolated = 10 + 10 * 0.95 = 19.5 → 20\n401\t/// assert_eq!(p95, 20); // Linear interpolation estimates p95\n402\t/// ```\n403\t///\n404\t/// ## Three elements (small sample)\n405\t///\n406\t/// ```\n407\t/// use needle::stats::calculate_p95;\n408\t///\n409\t/// let three = vec![10u128, 20, 30];\n410\t/// let p95 = calculate_p95(&three);\n411\t/// // rank = 0.95 * 2 = 1.9, floor_index = 1, fraction = 0.9\n412\t/// // floor_value = 20, ceiling_value = 30\n413\t/// // interpolated = 20 + 10 * 0.9 = 29.0 → 29\n414\t/// assert_eq!(p95, 29); // Linear interpolation estimates p95\n415\t/// ```\n416\t///\n417\t/// ## Real-world latency example\n418\t///\n419\t/// ```\n420\t/// use needle::stats::calculate_p95;\n421\t///\n422\t/// // Simulated latency data in milliseconds\n423\t/// let latencies = vec![\n424\t///     12, 15, 18, 20, 22, 25, 28, 30, 35, 40,\n425\t///     45, 50, 55, 60, 70, 80, 90, 100, 120, 150\n426\t/// ];\n427\t/// let p95 = calculate_p95(&latencies);\n428\t/// // rank = 0.95 * 19 = 18.05, floor_index = 18, fraction = 0.05\n429\t/// // floor_value = 120, ceiling_value = 150\n430\t/// // interpolated = 120 + 30 * 0.05 = 121.5 → 122\n431\t/// assert_eq!(p95, 122);\n432\t/// ```\n433\t///\n434\t/// # See Also\n435\t///\n436\t/// - [`docs/p95-calculation-algorithms.md`](../../docs/p95-calculation-algorithms.html) — Comprehensive survey of p95 algorithms and recommendations\n437\tpub fn calculate_p95(latencies: &[u128]) -> u128 {\n438\t    if latencies.is_empty() {\n439\t        return 0;\n440\t    }\n441\t\n442\t    let n = latencies.len();\n443\t    if n == 1 {\n444\t        return latencies[0];\n445\t    }\n446\t\n447\t    let mut sorted = Vec::from(latencies);\n448\t    sorted.sort();\n449\t\n450\t    // Linear interpolation method (like Criterion.rs)\n451\t    // Formula: rank = (p / 100) * (n - 1)\n452\t    // For p95: rank = 0.95 * (n - 1)\n453\t    let rank = 0.95 * (n - 1) as f64;\n454\t    let floor_index = rank.floor() as usize;\n455\t    let fraction = rank - floor_index as f64;\n456\t\n457\t    let floor_value = sorted[floor_index];\n458\t    let ceiling_value = sorted[floor_index + 1];\n459\t\n460\t    // Linear interpolation: floor + (ceiling - floor) * fraction\n461\t    let interpolated = floor_value as f64 + (ceiling_value - floor_value) as f64 * fraction;\n462\t\n463\t    // Round to nearest integer.\n464\t    // Add a small epsilon to handle floating point precision issues (e.g., 95.5 → 95.4999... → 95).\n465\t    // Standard rounding: 95.0-95.499... → 95, 95.5-96.499... → 96\n466\t    let epsilon = 1e-9;\n467\t    (interpolated + epsilon).round() as u128\n468\t}\n469\t\n470\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n471\t// P95 aggregation utilities\n472\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n473\t\n474\t/// Collector for aggregating samples across multiple benchmark iterations.\n475\t///\n476\t/// This struct provides a statistically sound way to aggregate latency measurements\n477\t/// across multiple iterations and calculate a single p95 percentile.\n478\t///\n479\t/// # Statistical Approach\n480\t///\n481\t/// The correct way to aggregate percentiles across multiple iterations is to:\n482\t/// 1. **Pool all samples** from all iterations into a single dataset\n483\t/// 2. **Calculate one p95** on the pooled data\n484\t///\n485\t/// **Do NOT average p95 values** from individual iterations — this is statistically\n486\t/// invalid because percentiles are non-linear statistics. Averaging them produces\n487\t/// misleading results.\n488\t///\n489\t/// # Example\n490\t///\n491\t/// ```no_run\n492\t/// use needle::stats::P95Collector;\n493\t/// use std::time::Instant;\n494\t///\n495\t/// let mut collector = P95Collector::new();\n496\t///\n497\t/// // Run benchmark for 50 iterations\n498\t/// for _ in 0..50 {\n499\t///     let start = Instant::now();\n500\t///     // ... perform work ...\n501\t///     collector.record(start.elapsed().as_micros());\n502\t/// }\n503\t///\n504\t/// // Calculate p95 across all iterations\n505\t/// let p95_us = collector.p95();\n506\t/// println!(\"p95 latency: {} μs\", p95_us);\n507\t/// ```\n508\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]\n509\tpub struct P95Collector {\n510\t    /// All recorded latency samples in microseconds.\n511\t    samples: Vec<u128>,\n512\t}\n513\t\n514\timpl P95Collector {\n515\t    /// Create a new empty collector.\n516\t    pub fn new() -> Self {\n517\t        Self {\n518\t            samples: Vec::new(),\n519\t        }\n520\t    }\n521\t\n522\t    /// Create a new collector with pre-allocated capacity.\n523\t    ///\n524\t    /// Use this when you know how many samples you'll collect to avoid\n525\t    /// reallocations during benchmarking.\n526\t    pub fn with_capacity(capacity: usize) -> Self {\n527\t        Self {\n528\t            samples: Vec::with_capacity(capacity),\n529\t        }\n530\t    }\n531\t\n532\t    /// Record a single latency sample in microseconds.\n533\t    pub fn record(&mut self, latency_us: u128) {\n534\t        self.samples.push(latency_us);\n535\t    }\n536\t\n537\t    /// Record multiple latency samples at once.\n538\t    pub fn record_all(&mut self, latencies: impl IntoIterator<Item = u128>) {\n539\t        self.samples.extend(latencies);\n540\t    }\n541\t\n542\t    /// Calculate the p95 percentile across all recorded samples.\n543\t    ///\n544\t    /// Returns `0` if no samples have been recorded.\n545\t    pub fn p95(&self) -> u128 {\n546\t        calculate_p95(&self.samples)\n547\t    }\n548\t\n549\t    /// Return the number of samples collected.\n550\t    pub fn count(&self) -> usize {\n551\t        self.samples.len()\n552\t    }\n553\t\n554\t    /// Clear all recorded samples.\n555\t    pub fn clear(&mut self) {\n556\t        self.samples.clear();\n557\t    }\n558\t\n559\t    /// Get a reference to the underlying samples.\n560\t    pub fn samples(&self) -> &[u128] {\n561\t        &self.samples\n562\t    }\n563\t\n564\t    /// Calculate additional statistics on the collected samples.\n565\t    ///\n566\t    /// Returns `(min, max, avg)` in microseconds, or `None` if no samples.\n567\t    pub fn stats(&self) -> Option<(u128, u128, f64)> {\n568\t        if self.samples.is_empty() {\n569\t            return None;\n570\t        }\n571\t        let min = *self.samples.iter().min().unwrap();\n572\t        let max = *self.samples.iter().max().unwrap();\n573\t        let sum: u128 = self.samples.iter().sum();\n574\t        let avg = sum as f64 / self.samples.len() as f64;\n575\t        Some((min, max, avg))\n576\t    }\n577\t}\n578\t\n579\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n580\t// Stats engine — multi-dimensional outcome aggregation\n581\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n582\t\n583\t/// Grouping dimension for the `needle stats` command.\n584\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]\n585\tpub enum StatsDimension {\n586\t    /// Group by template version tag (e.g., `\"pluck-v2\"`).\n587\t    TemplateVersion,\n588\t    /// Group by template name / task type (e.g., `\"pluck\"`).\n589\t    TaskType,\n590\t    /// Group by worker identifier (e.g., `\"needle-alpha\"`).\n591\t    Worker,\n592\t}\n593\t\n594\t/// Aggregated statistics row for one value of a grouping dimension.\n595\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]\n596\tpub struct StatsRow {\n597\t    /// The dimension value (version, task type, or worker id).\n598\t    pub key: String,\n599\t    /// Total number of beads dispatched in this group.\n600\t    pub beads: u64,\n601\t    /// Number of beads that completed with `\"Success\"` outcome.\n602\t    pub pass: u64,\n603\t    /// Number of beads that completed with `\"Failure\"` outcome.\n604\t    pub fail: u64,\n605\t    /// Number of beads that completed with `\"Timeout\"` outcome.\n606\t    pub timeout: u64,\n607\t    /// Sum of (tokens_in + tokens_out) across all effort events in this group.\n608\t    pub total_tokens: u64,\n609\t    /// Sum of `estimated_cost_usd` across all effort events in this group.\n610\t    pub total_cost_usd: f64,\n611\t    /// Number of effort events with token/cost data (denominator for averages).\n612\t    pub effort_events: u64,\n613\t}\n614\t\n615\timpl StatsRow {\n616\t    /// Pass rate as a fraction in `[0.0, 1.0]`. `None` when `beads == 0`.\n617\t    pub fn pass_rate(&self) -> Option<f64> {\n618\t        if self.beads == 0 {\n619\t            None\n620\t        } else {\n621\t            Some(self.pass as f64 / self.beads as f64)\n622\t        }\n623\t    }\n624\t\n625\t    /// Average total tokens (in + out) per effort event. `None` when no effort data.\n626\t    pub fn avg_tokens(&self) -> Option<f64> {\n627\t        if self.effort_events == 0 {\n628\t            None\n629\t        } else {\n630\t            Some(self.total_tokens as f64 / self.effort_events as f64)\n631\t        }\n632\t    }\n633\t\n634\t    /// Average cost in USD per effort event. `None` when no effort data.\n635\t    pub fn avg_cost_usd(&self) -> Option<f64> {\n636\t        if self.effort_events == 0 {\n637\t            None\n638\t        } else {\n639\t            Some(self.total_cost_usd / self.effort_events as f64)\n640\t        }\n641\t    }\n642\t}\n643\t\n644\t/// Compute per-group statistics from a pre-filtered slice of telemetry events.\n645\t///\n646\t/// Correlates `agent.dispatched`, `outcome.classified`, and `effort.recorded`\n647\t/// events by `bead_id`, grouping each bead under the chosen `dimension`.\n648\t///\n649\t/// Pass the result of [`telemetry::read_logs`] (already time-filtered) here.\n650\tpub fn compute_stats(\n651\t    events: &[crate::telemetry::TelemetryEvent],\n652\t    dimension: StatsDimension,\n653\t) -> Vec<StatsRow> {\n654\t    use std::collections::HashMap;\n655\t\n656\t    // bead_id → dimension key (populated from agent.dispatched events)\n657\t    let mut bead_key: HashMap<String, String> = HashMap::new();\n658\t    let mut rows: BTreeMap<String, StatsRow> = BTreeMap::new();\n659\t\n660\t    for event in events {\n661\t        match event.event_type.as_str() {\n662\t            \"agent.dispatched\" => {\n663\t                let bead_id = match event.bead_id.as_ref() {\n664\t                    Some(b) => b.as_ref().to_string(),\n665\t                    None => continue,\n666\t                };\n667\t                let key = match dimension {\n668\t                    StatsDimension::TemplateVersion => event\n669\t                        .data\n670\t                        .get(\"template_version\")\n671\t                        .and_then(|v| v.as_str())\n672\t                        .unwrap_or(\"unknown\")\n673\t                        .to_string(),\n674\t                    StatsDimension::TaskType => event\n675\t                        .data\n676\t                        .get(\"template_name\")\n677\t                        .and_then(|v| v.as_str())\n678\t                        .unwrap_or(\"unknown\")\n679\t                        .to_string(),\n680\t                    StatsDimension::Worker => event.worker_id.clone(),\n681\t                };\n682\t                bead_key.insert(bead_id, key.clone());\n683\t                let row = rows.entry(key.clone()).or_insert_with(|| StatsRow {\n684\t                    key: key.clone(),\n685\t                    ..Default::default()\n686\t                });\n687\t                row.beads += 1;\n688\t            }\n689\t\n690\t            \"outcome.classified\" => {\n691\t                let bead_id = match event.bead_id.as_ref() {\n692\t                    Some(b) => b.as_ref().to_string(),\n693\t                    None => continue,\n694\t                };\n695\t                if let Some(key) = bead_key.get(&bead_id) {\n696\t                    if let Some(row) = rows.get_mut(key) {\n697\t                        match event.data.get(\"outcome\").and_then(|v| v.as_str()) {\n698\t                            Some(\"Success\") => row.pass += 1,\n699\t                            Some(\"Failure\") => row.fail += 1,\n700\t                            Some(\"Timeout\") => row.timeout += 1,\n701\t                            _ => {}\n702\t                        }\n703\t                    }\n704\t                }\n705\t            }\n706\t\n707\t            \"effort.recorded\" => {\n708\t                let bead_id = match event.bead_id.as_ref() {\n709\t                    Some(b) => b.as_ref().to_string(),\n710\t                    None => continue,\n711\t                };\n712\t                if let Some(key) = bead_key.get(&bead_id) {\n713\t                    if let Some(row) = rows.get_mut(key) {\n714\t                        let tokens_in = event\n715\t                            .data\n716\t                            .get(\"tokens_in\")\n717\t                            .and_then(|v| v.as_u64())\n718\t                            .unwrap_or(0);\n719\t                        let tokens_out = event\n720\t                            .data\n721\t                            .get(\"tokens_out\")\n722\t                            .and_then(|v| v.as_u64())\n723\t                            .unwrap_or(0);\n724\t                        row.total_tokens += tokens_in + tokens_out;\n725\t                        if let Some(cost) = event\n726\t                            .data\n727\t                            .get(\"estimated_cost_usd\")\n728\t                            .and_then(|v| v.as_f64())\n729\t                        {\n730\t                            row.total_cost_usd += cost;\n731\t                        }\n732\t                        row.effort_events += 1;\n733\t                    }\n734\t                }\n735\t            }\n736\t\n737\t            _ => {}\n738\t        }\n739\t    }\n740\t\n741\t    rows.into_values().collect()\n742\t}\n743\t\n744\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n745\t// Tests\n746\t//       make_tel_event(\n1003\t                \"agent.dispatched\",\n1004\t                \"needle-alpha\",\n1005\t                Some(\"nd-1\"),\n1006\t Template versioning and A/B variant statistics.\n//!\n//! Reads `agent.dispatched` and `outcome.classified` telemetry events from\n//! JSONL log files and aggregates per-variant outcome counts and durations.\n//! Once enough beads have been dispatched (default: 50 per variant), a\n//! `VariantComparison` can be produced for the `needle stats` command.\n//!\n//! Leaf module — depends only on `types`.\n\nuse std::collections::BTreeMap;\nuse std::path::Path;\n\nuse anyhow::Result;\nuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Per-variant aggregates\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Aggregate statistics for one template variant.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]\npub struct VariantStats {\n    /// Variant version tag (e.g., `\"pluck-default\"`, `\"pluck-v2\"`).\n    pub version: String,\n    /// Total number of dispatches observed for this variant.\n    pub dispatches: u64,\n    /// Number of beads that completed with `\"Success\"` outcome.\n    pub successes: u64,\n    /// Number of beads that completed with `\"Failure\"` outcome.\n    pub failures: u64,\n    /// Number of beads that completed with `\"Timeout\"` outcome.\n    pub timeouts: u64,\n    /// Sum of dispatch durations in milliseconds (from `agent.completed`).\n    pub total_duration_ms: u64,\n}\n\nimpl VariantStats {\n    /// Success rate as a fraction in `[0.0, 1.0]`.  Returns `None` if no\n    /// dispatches have been recorded.\n    pub fn success_rate(&self) -> Option<f64> {\n        if self.dispatches == 0 {\n            None\n        } else {\n            Some(self.successes as f64 / self.dispatches as f64)\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Average dispatch duration in milliseconds.  Returns `None` if no\n    /// durations have been recorded.\n    pub fn avg_duration_ms(&self) -> Option<f64> {\n        if self.dispatches == 0 {\n            None\n        } else {\n            Some(self.total_duration_ms as f64 / self.dispatches as f64)\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Whether this variant has accumulated enough dispatches to be\n    /// considered statistically meaningful.\n    pub fn has_sufficient_data(&self, min_dispatches: u64) -> bool {\n        self.dispatches >= min_dispatches\n    }\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Comparison report\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Comparison of all variants for a single template name.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct VariantComparison {\n    /// Template name (e.g., `\"pluck\"`).\n    pub template_name: String,\n    /// Minimum dispatch count required before a variant is included.\n    pub min_dispatches: u64,\n    /// Stats for each variant, keyed by version tag.\n    pub variants: BTreeMap<String, VariantStats>,\n}\n\nimpl VariantComparison {\n    /// Returns variants that have at least `min_dispatches` observations.\n    pub fn ready_variants(&self) -> Vec<&VariantStats> {\n        self.variants\n            .values()\n            .filter(|v| v.has_sufficient_data(self.min_dispatches))\n            .collect()\n    }\n\n    /// Returns the variant with the highest success rate among ready variants,\n    /// or `None` if no variants are ready.\n    pub fn best_variant(&self) -> Option<&VariantStats> {\n        self.ready_variants().into_iter().max_by(|a, b| {\n            a.success_rate()\n                .unwrap_or(0.0)\n                .partial_cmp(&b.success_rate().unwrap_or(0.0))\n                .unwrap_or(std::cmp::Ordering::Equal)\n        })\n    }\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Aggregator\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Aggregates template variant statistics from JSONL telemetry log files.\n///\n/// # Usage\n///\n/// ```no_run\n/// use needle::stats::StatsAggregator;\n///\n/// let mut agg = StatsAggregator::new(50);\n/// agg.load_logs(std::path::Path::new(\"~/.needle/logs\")).unwrap();\n/// for (template, cmp) in agg.comparisons() {\n///     println!(\"{template}: {:?}\", cmp.best_variant());\n/// }\n/// ```\npub struct StatsAggregator {\n    /// Minimum dispatches per variant before comparisons are produced.\n    min_dispatches: u64,\n    /// Per-template, per-version stats.\n    ///\n    /// Outer key: template name (e.g., `\"pluck\"`).\n    /// Inner key: version tag (e.g., `\"pluck-v2\"`).\n    stats: BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, VariantStats>>,\n    /// Pending dispatch events waiting for an outcome, keyed by bead_id.\n    ///\n    /// Maps bead_id → (template_name, template_version).\n    pending: BTreeMap<String, (String, String)>,\n    /// Pending dispatch durations waiting for an `agent.completed` event,\n    /// keyed by bead_id.\n    pending_dispatch_start: BTreeMap<String, (String, String)>,\n}\n\nimpl StatsAggregator {\n    /// Create a new aggregator.\n    ///\n    /// `min_dispatches` — minimum observations per variant before a variant\n    /// is included in comparisons (default: 50).\n    pub fn new(min_dispatches: u64) -> Self {\n        StatsAggregator {\n            min_dispatches,\n            stats: BTreeMap::new(),\n            pending: BTreeMap::new(),\n            pending_dispatch_start: BTreeMap::new(),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Load and process all `*.jsonl` files under `log_dir`.\n    ///\n    /// Files are sorted by name (which sorts chronologically for date-prefixed\n    /// log files) so events are processed in order.\n    pub fn load_logs(&mut self, log_dir: &Path) -> Result<()> {\n        let mut paths: Vec<_> = std::fs::read_dir(log_dir)?\n            .filter_map(|e| e.ok())\n            .map(|e| e.path())\n            .filter(|p| p.extension().and_then(|e| e.to_str()) == Some(\"jsonl\"))\n            .collect();\n        paths.sort();\n\n        for path in paths {\n            self.load_file(&path)?;\n        }\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Load and process a single JSONL file.\n    pub fn load_file(&mut self, path: &Path) -> Result<()> {\n        let content = std::fs::read_to_string(path)?;\n        for line in content.lines() {\n            let line = line.trim();\n            if line.is_empty() {\n                continue;\n            }\n            if let Ok(event) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(line) {\n                self.process_event(&event);\n            }\n        }\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Process a single telemetry event JSON value.\n    fn process_event(&mut self, event: &serde_json::Value) {\n        let event_type = event.get(\"event_type\").and_then(|v| v.as_str());\n        let data = match event.get(\"data\") {\n            Some(d) => d,\n            None => return,\n        };\n\n        match event_type {\n            Some(\"agent.dispatched\") => {\n                let bead_id = data.get(\"bead_id\").and_then(|v| v.as_str());\n                let template_name = data.get(\"template_name\").and_then(|v| v.as_str());\n                let template_version = data.get(\"template_version\").and_then(|v| v.as_str());\n\n                if let (Some(bead_id), Some(name), Some(version)) =\n                    (bead_id, template_name, template_version)\n                {\n                    // Record a dispatch for this variant.\n                    let entry = self\n                        .stats\n                        .entry(name.to_string())\n                        .or_default()\n                        .entry(version.to_string())\n                        .or_insert_with(|| VariantStats {\n                            version: version.to_string(),\n                            ..Default::default()\n                        });\n                    entry.dispatches += 1;\n\n                    // Track pending for outcome correlation.\n                    self.pending\n                        .insert(bead_id.to_string(), (name.to_string(), version.to_string()));\n                    self.pending_dispatch_start\n                        .insert(bead_id.to_string(), (name.to_string(), version.to_string()));\n                }\n            }\n\n            Some(\"agent.completed\") => {\n                let bead_id = data.get(\"bead_id\").and_then(|v| v.as_str());\n                let duration_ms = data.get(\"duration_ms\").and_then(|v| v.as_u64());\n\n                if let (Some(bead_id), Some(duration)) = (bead_id, duration_ms) {\n                    if let Some((name, version)) = self.pending_dispatch_start.remove(bead_id) {\n                        if let Some(entry) =\n                            self.stats.get_mut(&name).and_then(|m| m.get_mut(&version))\n                        {\n                            entry.total_duration_ms += duration;\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            Some(\"outcome.classified\") => {\n                let bead_id = data.get(\"bead_id\").and_then(|v| v.as_str());\n                let outcome = data.get(\"outcome\").and_then(|v| v.as_str());\n\n                if let (Some(bead_id), Some(outcome)) = (bead_id, outcome) {\n                    if let Some((name, version)) = self.pending.remove(bead_id) {\n                        if let Some(entry) =\n                            self.stats.get_mut(&name).and_then(|m| m.get_mut(&version))\n                        {\n                            match outcome {\n                                \"Success\" => entry.successes += 1,\n                                \"Failure\" => entry.failures += 1,\n                                \"Timeout\" => entry.timeouts += 1,\n                                _ => {}\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            _ => {}\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Produce a `VariantComparison` for every template that has been observed.\n    pub fn comparisons(&self) -> BTreeMap<String, VariantComparison> {\n        self.stats\n            .iter()\n            .map(|(template_name, by_version)| {\n                let comparison = VariantComparison {\n                    template_name: template_name.clone(),\n                    min_dispatches: self.min_dispatches,\n                    variants: by_version.clone(),\n                };\n                (template_name.clone(), comparison)\n            })\n            .collect()\n    }\n\n    /// Produce a comparison for a specific template name, or `None` if no\n    /// events have been observed for that template.\n    pub fn comparison_for(&self, template_name: &str) -> Option<VariantComparison> {\n        self.stats\n            .get(template_name)\n            .map(|by_version| VariantComparison {\n                template_name: template_name.to_string(),\n                min_dispatches: self.min_dispatches,\n                variants: by_version.clone(),\n            })\n    }\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Percentile helpers\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Calculate the 95th percentile from a slice of values.\n///\n/// The 95th percentile (p95) is the value below which 95% of observations\n/// fall. It is commonly used to understand the \"tail\" of a distribution\n/// — for example, in latency metrics, p95 tells you that 95% of requests\n/// completed within this time, while the slowest 5% took longer.\n///\n/// # Edge Cases\n///\n/// This function handles all edge cases gracefully:\n///\n/// - **Empty slice**: Returns `0` (no data available)\n/// - **Single element**: Returns that element (the only value available)\n/// - **Two elements**: Uses linear interpolation to estimate p95 between the two values\n/// - **Small samples (2-3 elements)**: Linear interpolation provides a reasonable estimate\n///\n/// All edge cases return sensible results without panicking.\n///\n/// # Algorithm\n///\n/// This function uses **linear interpolation**, which is the same method\n/// used by Criterion.rs and is more accurate than the nearest-rank method:\n///\n/// 1. If the slice is empty, return 0 (no data case)\n/// 2. If the slice has one element, return that element\n/// 3. Sort the values in ascending order\n/// 4. Calculate the rank: `rank = 0.95 * (n - 1)` where `n` is the number of elements\n/// 5. Split the rank into integer and fractional parts\n/// 6. Return the linear interpolation: `floor_value + (ceiling_value - floor_value) * fraction`\n/// 7. Round to the nearest integer for the final result\n///\n/// This method was chosen because:\n/// - **Accurate**: Uses linear interpolation like Criterion.rs for smooth percentile estimates\n/// - **Standard**: Matches the behavior of common benchmarking libraries\n/// - **Well-documented**: The algorithm is described in statistical literature\n/// - **Deterministic**: Always produces the same result for the same input\n/// - **Handles all sample sizes**: Works correctly from 0 to very large datasets\n///\n/// Note: This uses linear interpolation, not nearest-rank. For example, with 10 elements\n/// `[10, 20, ..., 100]`, the 95th percentile is approximately `95.5` (rounded to `96`),\n/// not the maximum value `100`.\n///\n/// # Examples\n///\n/// ## Basic usage with sorted data\n///\n/// ```\n/// use needle::stats::calculate_p95;\n///\n/// let latencies = vec![10u128, 20, 30, 40, 50, 60, 70, 80, 90, 100];\n/// let p95 = calculate_p95(&latencies);\n/// // rank = 0.95 * 9 = 8.55, floor_index = 8, fraction = 0.55\n/// // floor_value = 90, ceiling_value = 100\n/// // interpolated = 90 + 10 * 0.55 = 95.5 → 96\n/// assert_eq!(p95, 96);\n/// ```\n///\n/// ## Works with unsorted input (sorts internally)\n///\n/// ```\n/// use needle::stats::calculate_p95;\n///\n/// let unsorted = vec![100u128, 10, 50, 30, 70, 40, 60, 20, 80, 90];\n/// let p95 = calculate_p95(&unsorted);\n/// assert_eq!(p95, 96); // Function sorts internally → same result\n/// ```\n///\n/// ## Larger dataset\n///\n/// ```\n/// use needle::stats::calculate_p95;\n///\n/// // 20 elements: rank = 0.95 * 19 = 18.05, floor_index = 18, fraction = 0.05\n/// let data = vec![10u128, 20, 30, 40, 50, 60, 70, 80, 90, 100,\n///                  110, 120, 130, 140, 150, 160, 170, 180, 190, 200];\n/// let p95 = calculate_p95(&data);\n/// // floor_value = 190, ceiling_value = 200\n/// // interpolated = 190 + 10 * 0.05 = 190.5 → 191\n/// assert_eq!(p95, 191);\n/// ```\n///\n/// ## Single element (degenerate case)\n///\n/// ```\n/// use needle::stats::calculate_p95;\n///\n/// let single = vec![42u128];\n/// let p95 = calculate_p95(&single);\n/// assert_eq!(p95, 42); // Only value available\n/// ```\n///\n/// ## Empty input\n///\n/// ```\n/// use needle::stats::calculate_p95;\n///\n/// let empty: Vec<u128> = vec![];\n/// let p95 = calculate_p95(&empty);\n/// assert_eq!(p95, 0); // No data → returns 0\n/// ```\n///\n/// ## Two elements (small sample)\n///\n/// ```\n/// use needle::stats::calculate_p95;\n///\n/// let two = vec![10u128, 20];\n/// let p95 = calculate_p95(&two);\n/// // rank = 0.95 * 1 = 0.95, floor_index = 0, fraction = 0.95\n/// // floor_value = 10, ceiling_value = 20\n/// // interpolated = 10 + 10 * 0.95 = 19.5 → 20\n/// assert_eq!(p95, 20); // Linear interpolation estimates p95\n/// ```\n///\n/// ## Three elements (small sample)\n///\n/// ```\n/// use needle::stats::calculate_p95;\n///\n/// let three = vec![10u128, 20, 30];\n/// let p95 = calculate_p95(&three);\n/// // rank = 0.95 * 2 = 1.9, floor_index = 1, fraction = 0.9\n/// // floor_value = 20, ceiling_value = 30\n/// // interpolated = 20 + 10 * 0.9 = 29.0 → 29\n/// assert_eq!(p95, 29); // Linear interpolation estimates p95\n/// ```\n///\n/// ## Real-world latency example\n///\n/// ```\n/// use needle::stats::calculate_p95;\n///\n/// // Simulated latency data in milliseconds\n/// let latencies = vec![\n///     12, 15, 18, 20, 22, 25, 28, 30, 35, 40,\n///     45, 50, 55, 60, 70, 80, 90, 100, 120, 150\n/// ];\n/// let p95 = calculate_p95(&latencies);\n/// // rank = 0.95 * 19 = 18.05, floor_index = 18, fraction = 0.05\n/// // floor_value = 120, ceiling_value = 150\n/// // interpolated = 120 + 30 * 0.05 = 121.5 → 122\n/// assert_eq!(p95, 122);\n/// ```\n///\n/// # See Also\n///\n/// - [`docs/p95-calculation-algorithms.md`](../../docs/p95-calculation-algorithms.html) — Comprehensive survey of p95 algorithms and recommendations\npub fn calculate_p95(latencies: &[u128]) -> u128 {\n    if latencies.is_empty() {\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    let n = latencies.len();\n    if n == 1 {\n        return latencies[0];\n    }\n\n    let mut sorted = Vec::from(latencies);\n    sorted.sort();\n\n    // Linear interpolation method (like Criterion.rs)\n    // Formula: rank = (p / 100) * (n - 1)\n    // For p95: rank = 0.95 * (n - 1)\n    let rank = 0.95 * (n - 1) as f64;\n    let floor_index = rank.floor() as usize;\n    let fraction = rank - floor_index as f64;\n\n    let floor_value = sorted[floor_index];\n    let ceiling_value = sorted[floor_index + 1];\n\n    // Linear interpolation: floor + (ceiling - floor) * fraction\n    let interpolated = floor_value as f64 + (ceiling_value - floor_value) as f64 * fraction;\n\n    // Round to nearest integer.\n    // Add a small epsilon to handle floating point precision issues (e.g., 95.5 → 95.4999... → 95).\n    // Standard rounding: 95.0-95.499... → 95, 95.5-96.499... → 96\n    let epsilon = 1e-9;\n    (interpolated + epsilon).round() as u128\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// P95 aggregation utilities\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Collector for aggregating samples across multiple benchmark iterations.\n///\n/// This struct provides a statistically sound way to aggregate latency measurements\n/// across multiple iterations and calculate a single p95 percentile.\n///\n/// # Statistical Approach\n///\n/// The correct way to aggregate percentiles across multiple iterations is to:\n/// 1. **Pool all samples** from all iterations into a single dataset\n/// 2. **Calculate one p95** on the pooled data\n///\n/// **Do NOT average p95 values** from individual iterations — this is statistically\n/// invalid because percentiles are non-linear statistics. Averaging them produces\n/// misleading results.\n///\n/// # Example\n///\n/// ```no_run\n/// use needle::stats::P95Collector;\n/// use std::time::Instant;\n///\n/// let mut collector = P95Collector::new();\n///\n/// // Run benchmark for 50 iterations\n/// for _ in 0..50 {\n///     let start = Instant::now();\n///     // ... perform work ...\n///     collector.record(start.elapsed().as_micros());\n/// }\n///\n/// // Calculate p95 across all iterations\n/// let p95_us = collector.p95();\n/// println!(\"p95 latency: {} μs\", p95_us);\n/// ```\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]\npub struct P95Collector {\n    /// All recorded latency samples in microseconds.\n    samples: Vec<u128>,\n}\n\nimpl P95Collector {\n    /// Create a new empty collector.\n    pub fn new() -> Self {\n        Self {\n            samples: Vec::new(),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Create a new collector with pre-allocated capacity.\n    ///\n    /// Use this when you know how many samples you'll collect to avoid\n    /// reallocations during benchmarking.\n    pub fn with_capacity(capacity: usize) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            samples: Vec::with_capacity(capacity),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Record a single latency sample in microseconds.\n    pub fn record(&mut self, latency_us: u128) {\n        self.samples.push(latency_us);\n    }\n\n    /// Record multiple latency samples at once.\n    pub fn record_all(&mut self, latencies: impl IntoIterator<Item = u128>) {\n        self.samples.extend(latencies);\n    }\n\n    /// Calculate the p95 percentile across all recorded samples.\n    ///\n    /// Returns `0` if no samples have been recorded.\n    pub fn p95(&self) -> u128 {\n        calculate_p95(&self.samples)\n    }\n\n    /// Return the number of samples collected.\n    pub fn count(&self) -> usize {\n        self.samples.len()\n    }\n\n    /// Clear all recorded samples.\n    pub fn clear(&mut self) {\n        self.samples.clear();\n    }\n\n    /// Get a reference to the underlying samples.\n    pub fn samples(&self) -> &[u128] {\n        &self.samples\n    }\n\n    /// Calculate additional statistics on the collected samples.\n    ///\n    /// Returns `(min, max, avg)` in microseconds, or `None` if no samples.\n    pub fn stats(&self) -> Option<(u128, u128, f64)> {\n        if self.samples.is_empty() {\n            return None;\n        }\n        let min = *self.samples.iter().min().unwrap();\n        let max = *self.samples.iter().max().unwrap();\n        let sum: u128 = self.samples.iter().sum();\n        let avg = sum as f64 / self.samples.len() as f64;\n        Some((min, max, avg))\n    }\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Stats engine — multi-dimensional outcome aggregation\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Grouping dimension for the `needle stats` command.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub enum StatsDimension {\n    /// Group by template version tag (e.g., `\"pluck-v2\"`).\n    TemplateVersion,\n    /// Group by template name / task type (e.g., `\"pluck\"`).\n    TaskType,\n    /// Group by worker identifier (e.g., `\"needle-alpha\"`).\n    Worker,\n}\n\n/// Aggregated statistics row for one value of a grouping dimension.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]\npub struct StatsRow {\n    /// The dimension value (version, task type, or worker id).\n    pub key: String,\n    /// Total number of beads dispatched in this group.\n    pub beads: u64,\n    /// Number of beads that completed with `\"Success\"` outcome.\n    pub pass: u64,\n    /// Number of beads that completed with `\"Failure\"` outcome.\n    pub fail: u64,\n    /// Number of beads that completed with `\"Timeout\"` outcome.\n    pub timeout: u64,\n    /// Sum of (tokens_in + tokens_out) across all effort events in this group.\n    pub total_tokens: u64,\n    /// Sum of `estimated_cost_usd` across all effort events in this group.\n    pub total_cost_usd: f64,\n    /// Number of effort events with token/cost data (denominator for averages).\n    pub effort_events: u64,\n}\n\nimpl StatsRow {\n    /// Pass rate as a fraction in `[0.0, 1.0]`. `None` when `beads == 0`.\n    pub fn pass_rate(&self) -> Option<f64> {\n        if self.beads == 0 {\n            None\n        } else {\n            Some(self.pass as f64 / self.beads as f64)\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Average total tokens (in + out) per effort event. `None` when no effort data.\n    pub fn avg_tokens(&self) -> Option<f64> {\n        if self.effort_events == 0 {\n            None\n        } else {\n            Some(self.total_tokens as f64 / self.effort_events as f64)\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Average cost in USD per effort event. `None` when no effort data.\n    pub fn avg_cost_usd(&self) -> Option<f64> {\n        if self.effort_events == 0 {\n            None\n        } else {\n            Some(self.total_cost_usd / self.effort_events as f64)\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Compute per-group statistics from a pre-filtered slice of telemetry events.\n///\n/// Correlates `agent.dispatched`, `outcome.classified`, and `effort.recorded`\n/// events by `bead_id`, grouping each bead under the chosen `dimension`.\n///\n/// Pass the result of [`telemetry::read_logs`] (already time-filtered) here.\npub fn compute_stats(\n    events: &[crate::telemetry::TelemetryEvent],\n    dimension: StatsDimension,\n) -> Vec<StatsRow> {\n    use std::collections::HashMap;\n\n    // bead_id → dimension key (populated from agent.dispatched events)\n    let mut bead_key: HashMap<String, String> = HashMap::new();\n    let mut rows: BTreeMap<String, StatsRow> = BTreeMap::new();\n\n    for event in events {\n        match event.event_type.as_str() {\n            \"agent.dispatched\" => {\n                let bead_id = match event.bead_id.as_ref() {\n                    Some(b) => b.as_ref().to_string(),\n                    None => continue,\n                };\n                let key = match dimension {\n                    StatsDimension::TemplateVersion => event\n                        .data\n                        .get(\"template_version\")\n                        .and_then(|v| v.as_str())\n                        .unwrap_or(\"unknown\")\n                        .to_string(),\n                    StatsDimension::TaskType => event\n                        .data\n                        .get(\"template_name\")\n                        .and_then(|v| v.as_str())\n                        .unwrap_or(\"unknown\")\n                        .to_string(),\n                    StatsDimension::Worker => event.worker_id.clone(),\n                };\n                bead_key.insert(bead_id, key.clone());\n                let row = rows.entry(key.clone()).or_insert_with(|| StatsRow {\n                    key: key.clone(),\n                    ..Default::default()\n                });\n                row.beads += 1;\n            }\n\n            \"outcome.classified\" => {\n                let bead_id = match event.bead_id.as_ref() {\n                    Some(b) => b.as_ref().to_string(),\n                    None => continue,\n                };\n                if let Some(key) = bead_key.get(&bead_id) {\n                    if let Some(row) = rows.get_mut(key) {\n                        match event.data.get(\"outcome\").and_then(|v| v.as_str()) {\n                            Some(\"Success\") => row.pass += 1,\n                            Some(\"Failure\") => row.fail += 1,\n                            Some(\"Timeout\") => row.timeout += 1,\n                            _ => {}\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            \"effort.recorded\" => {\n                let bead_id = match event.bead_id.as_ref() {\n                    Some(b) => b.as_ref().to_string(),\n                    None => continue,\n                };\n                if let Some(key) = bead_key.get(&bead_id) {\n                    if let Some(row) = rows.get_mut(key) {\n                        let tokens_in = event\n                            .data\n                            .get(\"tokens_in\")\n                            .and_then(|v| v.as_u64())\n                            .unwrap_or(0);\n                        let tokens_out = event\n                            .data\n                            .get(\"tokens_out\")\n                            .and_then(|v| v.as_u64())\n                            .unwrap_or(0);\n                        row.total_tokens += tokens_in + tokens_out;\n                        if let Some(cost) = event\n                            .data\n                            .get(\"estimated_cost_usd\")\n                            .and_then(|v| v.as_f64())\n                        {\n                            row.total_cost_usd += cost;\n                        }\n                        row.effort_events += 1;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            _ => {}\n        }\n    }\n\n    rows.into_values().collect()\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Tests\n// worker_id: &str,\n        bead_id: Option<&str>,\n        data: serde_json::Value,\n    ) -> crate::telemetry::TelemetryEvent {\n        crate::telemetry::TelemetryEvent {\n            timestamp: chrono::Utc::now(),\n            event_type: event_type.to_string(),\n            worker_id: worker_id.to_string(),\n            session_id: \"test0000\".to_string(),\n            sequence: 0,\n            bead_id: bead_id.map(crate::types::BeadId::from),\n            workspace: None,\n            data,\n            duration_ms: None,\n            trace_id: None,\n            span_id: None,\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn compute_stats_by_template_version() {\n        let events = vec![\n            make_tel_event(\n                \"agent.dispatched\",\n                \"needle-alpha\",\n                Some(\"nd-1\"),\n                serde_json::json!({\"template_name\": \"pluck\", \"template_version\": \"pluck-v1\"}),\n            ),\n            make_tel_event(\n                \"outcome.classified\",\n                \"needle-alpha\",\n                Some(\"nd-1\"),\n                serde_json::json!({\"outcome\": \"Success\"}),\n            ),\n            make_tel_event(\n                \"effort.recorded\",\n                \"needle-alpha\",\n                Some(\"nd-1\"),\n                serde_json::json!({\"tokens_in\": 100, \"tokens_out\": 50, \"estimated_cost_usd\": 0.01}),\n            ),\n            make_tel_event(\n                \"agent.dispatched\",\n                \"needle-alpha\",\n                Some(\"nd-2\"),\n                serde_json::json!({\"template_name\": \"pluck\", \"template_version\": \"pluck-v2\"}),\n            ),\n            make_tel_event(\n                \"outcome.classified\",\n                \"needle-alpha\",\n                Some(\"nd-2\"),\n                serde_json::json!({\"outcome\": \"Failure\"}),\n            ),\n        ];\n\n        let mut rows = compute_stats(&events, StatsDimension::TemplateVersion);\n        rows.sort_by(|a, b| a.key.cmp(&b.key));\n\n        assert_eq!(rows.len(), 2);\n\n        let v1 = rows.iter().find(|r| r.key == \"pluck-v1\").unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(v1.beads, 1);\n        assert_eq!(v1.pass, 1);\n        assert_eq!(v1.fail, 0);\n        assert_eq!(v1.total_tokens, 150);\n    #[test]\n    fn calculate_p95_single_element() {\n        let data = vec![42u128];\n        assert_eq!(calculate_p95(&data), 42);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn calculate_p95_sorted() {\n        let data = vec![10u128, 20, 30, 40, 50, 60, 70, 80, 90, 100];\n        // Linear interpolation: rank = 0.95 * 9 = 8.55, floor=8, frac=0.55\n        // 90 + (100-90) * 0.55 = 95.5 → 96\n        assert_eq!(calculate_p95(&data), 96);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn calculate_p95_unsorted() {\n        let data = vec![100u128, 10, 50, 30, 70, 40, 60, 20, 80, 90];\n        // Same as sorted test after internal sorting\n        assert_eq!(calculate_p95(&data), 96);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn calculate_p95_twenty_elements() {\n        let data = vec![\n            10u128, 20, 30, 40, 50, 60, 70, 80, 90, 100, 110, 120, 130, 140, 150, 160, 170, 180,\n            190, 200,\n        ];\n        // Linear interpolation: rank = 0.95 * 19 = 18.05, floor=18, frac=0.05\n        // 190 + (200-190) * 0.05 = 190.5 → 191\n        assert_eq!(calculate_p95(&data), 191);\n    }\n\n    // ── P95Collector tests ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    #[test]\n    fn p95_collector_empty() {\n        let collector = P95Collector::new();\n        assert_eq!(collector.p95(), 0);\n        assert_eq!(collector.count(), 0);\n        assert!(collector.stats().is_none());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn p95_collector_single_sample() {\n        let mut collector = P95Collector::new();\n        collector.record(42);\n        assert_eq!(collector.p95(), 42);\n        assert_eq!(collector.count(), 1);\n        let stats = collector.stats().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(stats.0, 42); // min\n        assert_eq!(stats.1, 42); // max\n        assert_eq!(stats.2, 42.0); // avg\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn p95_collector_multiple_samples() {\n        let mut collector = P95Collector::new();\n        for i in 1..=10 {\n            collector.record(i * 10);\n        }\n        assert_eq!(collector.count(), 10);\n        // Should match calculate_p95 on the same data\n        let data: Vec<u128> = (1..=10).map(|i| i * 10).collect();\n        assert_eq!(collector.p95(), calculate_p95(&data));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn p95_collector_with_capacity() {\n        let mut collector = P95Collector::with_capacity(100);\n        assert_eq!(collector.count(), 0);\n        // Should not reallocate\n        for i in 0..100 {\n            collector.record(i);\n        }\n        assert_eq!(collector.count(), 100);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn p95_collector_record_all() {\n        let mut collector = P95Collector::new();\n        let samples = vec![10u128, 20, 30, 40, 50];\n p95_collector_stats() {\n        let mut collector = P95Collector::new();\n        collector.record(10);\n        collector.record(20);\n        collector.record(30);\n\n        let stats = collector.stats().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(stats.0, 10); // min\n        assert_eq!(stats.1, 30); // max\n        assert_eq!(stats.2, 20.0); // avg = (10+20+30)/3\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn p95_collector_clear() {\n        let mut collector = P95Collector::new();\n        collector.record(100);\n        collector.record(200);\n        assert_eq!(collector.count(), 2);\n\n
